{0} to {1} Reward
There are 6 divisions (Bronze, Silver, Gold, Platinum, Diamond, Heroic), each with some sub-divisions for you to progress. Achievements such as getting high placing in matches, eliminating enemies, reviving teammates, dealing damage will all increase the RP you get from each match. In Duo or Squad mode, if you quit the game before the team result is decided, your RP will only be determined by the actions before you quit.
When your RP reaches a certain level, you will be promoted to the next division. Likewise, if your RP drops to a certain level, you will be demoted to a lower division.
As you progress along each division, you will receive rewards (one-time only). When the season ends, season rewards will be given according to the highest division you achieved in the entire season.
Ranked leaderboards are sorted by division and sub-divisions. You can see how you place amongst your friends. If your rank is high enough, you may even find yourself in the global leaderboards.
At the end of each season, rank will be reset. Depending on your performance in the previous season, you might begin at a higher rank when the new season begins.

Name: Maxim\nGender: Male\nAge: 17\nDate of birth: 30 November\n
Maxim! Why is there nothing left in the fridge!?\nSis... I was just feeling a little... hungry...\n... Ok. I can't believe you finished everything. I'm going for a run later. \n-You are really eating too much, our daily expenses are getting so high... \nI need to think of a way to earn some money... Misha must be feeling stressed out... \n
Even while I'm taking a jog... I'm hungry... \nEh? That yellow figure looks familiar... ah! Kelly! She's cute... I'm gonna say hi! \nMaxim runs over to Kelly. \nHi Kelly! I... \n*Stomach gurgling* Omg... that was loud... and in front of Kelly! Kill me now... \nOh Maxim, I was just going to eat, want to join me? \nWhat? Of course!
Hey Kelly what do you like to eat? \nRamen! \nKelly led me to the side of the street. \n New item promotion! Finish 10 bowls of hellfire spicy ramen, and enjoy all year round discounts in the stall! Pity, I can't take spicy. \nKelly frowned.\n Perhaps... I can try? \nYou? \nHehe, actually.. I can really eat. \n Watching her shocked expression as I finish the 10 bowls of ramen... \n
After shocking the stall owner with my humongous appetite, he suggested I could livestream myself eating? Perhaps I could even earn some quick bucks... \nThe more important thing is... Kelly also encouraged me to do so! \nWhat are you doing Maxim! What are you doing with so many pizzas and apple pies? \n Erm... Erm... To eat of course.... \nAfter ushering Misha out of the room, Maxim went in front of the camera. \nHey everyone! My name is Maxim, I'm a student, and I'm good at... eating! So today I will bring you guys my first livestream, and a review of the 15 best pizzas in the city!
Sister! Kelly liked my stream again! \nKelly... Kelly... you say the name at least 10 times a day! \nHehe... do I? \nYou like her? \nMisha smirks at Maxim. \nMaxim blushes uncontrollably. \nHaha! I can see everything on your face. Ask her out on a date! \nI'll try... \n
Welcome to Purgatory, Maxim. Will you be the last man standing?
I finally got the courage to head to Kelly's house, but nobody's here! \nWhere did Kelly go?! \nI waited till midnight... \nWhen I reached home, Maxim realized Misha wasn't back either. \nThis could be the strangest day of Maxim's life yet. He missed his crush, and his sister was nowhere to be found. \n

Misha's Records G
Today is Caroline's birthday and she'll be holding a party tonight.\nCaroline's dad was always busy with work at the lab and Caroline rarely sees him. He said he would try his best to make it for the party though.\nHe made it, just when the clock ticked 12.\nThey hugged, and in that moment I thought about my Dad. I miss him.\nCaroline's dad waved to me.\nWhen he saw me, he froze..

Hip-hop Badge
Kla
Name: Kla\nGender: Male\nAged 27\nBirthday: December 14th\n
Konkun is big and fierce, but definitely no match for me.\nWith a simple feint followed by a mighty jumping kick, I easily drew Konkun's blood.\nShow him no mercy! Finish him! Konkun stood there, dazed and bleeding from the lips.\n\nI shook my head in disappointment and walked out of this dirty place, which reeked of alochol, sweat and bad perfume.
Right at this moment, a tall and skinny blonde man walked into the changing room.\nWhat do you want?\nIf my memory serves me well, this is your 423rd day participating in underground fights.\nWhat do you want? I say again.\nI come in peace.\nHe sat down on the large sofa behind him.\nI have a proposition for you, one that can restore you to your former glory.
That's right, I lost many things.\nAll those things coveted by everyone.\nIn Muay Thai, Karate, MMA, I swept all those trophies even as a kid.\nUntil that one day Somchai, my manager, gave me an offer that did not sit well with me.\nHe wanted me to pretend to get beaten to a pulp.\nNever!\nWake up Kla, it's that guy, the boss.\nSomchai, I need a new manager.
What happened after?\nI'm sorry, Kla. I could'nt help. The test was positive. All your wins will be taken from you and you will have to go to prison.\nSomchai, you know I would never use steroids. Get me the best lawyers!\nAll the evidence points towards you, if you plead guilty, you could have a lighter sentence.\nGoodbye, Kla.\nI could see a smile forming in the corner of his lips.
Mr. Kla, have you reconsidered?\n\nThe man's smile disgusted me, and I could feel my heartbeat getting faster.\nA voice in my head pleaded with me to accept his offer and take back all that belonged to me.\nMy honor, my status and my riches. Somchai took them all away from me. Now all I am left with is hate and these underground fights.\nI can't stay here forever. What do you want with me?
Welcome to Purgatory, Kla! Will you be able to find that thing you lost?
On this island, I have gotten used to killing people during every waking moment.\nEverytime I kill, I get reminded of Somchai, Thongchai and all the other victims.\nNobody is innocent, as long as they get in the way of my revenge.\nI do not have a choice.\nI always wondered, between match fixing and killing people, which is the lesser evil?

Paloma
Written Notes (Paloma)
Name: Paloma\nFemale\nAged 28\nBirthday: Aug 4
Cute bear plushie\nThe plushie's legs were ripped off and neatly sewed up.\nIt is difficult to imagine the famous arms queen of the underworld would keep such a sentimental toy with her for so long.\nAs she lit up a cigarette, she shared that they were living in the poorest sector of the city. One time, some thugs wanted to steal it and Miguel tried to fight them off and ended up being badly injured.
Wilted rose\nIt was Feb 14th, Miguel got into the police academy and came to say his farewells to me.\nPaloma, come with me, he said.\nWhy are you still with that guy? He was the sort of guy who would bully us when we were younger.\nNo, that is just the sort of protection I need. People like us need protection.\nMiguel gave me a dove-shaped pin and we went our separate ways.
Rusty bullet casing\nI was still wearing my tiara from the pageant when I reached the hospital.\nAn hour ago, I was still soaking in the happiness of becoming the next beauty queen. I thought this would be the opportunity we needed to break out of our criminal lifestyle.\nMy beloved lover just abandoned me, only leaving me with a blood-stained bullet.\nPlease stop crying, Paloma, unless you do not want to avenge your lover.\nOnly with your help can we execute this plan. Please get yourself together.
Old news article\nBeauty Queen Turned Felon.\nPaloma's rise from poverty into a beauty queenis a story that most people are familiar with. Today, she is now one of the most powerful arms dealer.\nReliable sources state that her boyfriend used to be one of the top dogs in the mafia, who disappeared without a trace.\nShe replaced him shortly after as the leader of organized crime. Most notably, the biggest arms trafficking business in the world. Her moniker - Arms Queen.
A mysterious letter\nA corpse lay in the armory. He was the 19th person to assassinate Miguel. Still a long way before my revenge is completed. I will find them, and destroy them.\nSeven years later, the world sees me as the worst criminal to have ever walked this Earth. I will never stop until I find him.\nToday, I received a letter that contains his handwriting.\nMy dear Paloma, forgive me for not writing to you sooner. I am now in...
Welcome to Purgatory, Paloma! Can you find your long lost lover?
A contract\nTo be honest, I have a large business deal for you, Paloma.\nI have no time for games! Where is he?\nPlease be patient. Your pretty face is ruined when you are flustered.\nIf you are willing to work with me, like how you worked with them 7 years ago. We can negotiate a way for you to meet who you want to meet.\nRight then, the windows broke and there was gunfire, explosions, choppers all around.\nIs it him?!

Congratulations, you joined a guild!
1. Level up guilds by accumulating Glory. The higher the level, the more privileges the whole guild can enjoy.\n2. Guild privileges include: additional gold and experience,  daily gold limit increased and guild member limit increased.\n3. Players can gain glory by playing games or through diamond top-up:\na. Glory gained after each game will depend on your placing in the match;\nb. Playing games with your guild mates, will get you 4 glory, with a daily cap of 80;\nc. For every 10 diamonds purchased, you will get 1 glory.\n4. When a player leaves a guild, his glory will be reset.\n5. The amount of glory that a guild can get in a day is capped, proportionate to the maximum number of members the guild can have. When the daily cap is reached, the guild will no longer receive any additional glory for the day.
1. Guild size is limited to 20 people at first. This limit can be increased by leveling up the guild or purchasing a guild expansion. \n2. Every guild can have 1 leader and 3 elders.\n3. Guild leaders can: appoint or demote elders, approve guild requests, kick members, change the slogan and announcement, change the guild name and expand the guild. Elders can: approve guild requests, kick members, change the slogan and announcement and expand the guild.\n4. Leaders and elders can invite their friends and other players into the guild, with a maximum of 30 invites. Players can invite their guild mates to a squad.\n5. Players may not leave a guild within 24 hours of joining.

Olivia\n\nFemale\nAged 29\nDate of Birth: 11th Oct
Have you seen Olivia?\nThe charming nurse?\nYes, the injections will hurt less if she's around.\nOh yeah, wish I could spend some time in bed with her at night.\nHehe, I'm telling Olivia!\nThe two playful kids ran around the block.\n\nMy name is Olivia, the chief nurse.\nAs long as you behave yourself, the injections will not hurt one bit.
Already ten patients. Symptoms: hair loss, abnormal blood samples, weakened immune system.\nThey all spend long hours at sea: water policemen, ship captains, fishermen... And on the second day, all of them will escape from the hospital.\n\nI'm worried about Ford.\nHe has been out at sea for three months now.
A firefight broke out in the city and the injured are still streaming into the hospital. The operating theatres are full of injured civillians.\nBy the time this wounded policeman reached the hospital, we had no blood left for transfusion.\n\nThank god that I'm also blood type B.\nAfter a 700cc blood transfusion from me, he was saved.\n\nFord, where are you? I'm scared.
Ford finally proposed! After being at sea for five months, he was pale, but still his eyes and that ring he had were still shining brightly.\nThere were still many patients, but I decided to leave for my honeymoon after that policeman was discharged. Ford, my husband, also promised to spend more time with me instead of going out to sea.\n\nLife was so perfect.
Save me, lord!\nGuns were going off inside the hospital. Blood splattered across the floor, the walls, and the ceiling.\nThere was a little kid crying in the corridor. I have to take him to safety.\nWait, do I hear Ford's voice? Why are you here?\n BANG!\nThat was the very last sound I heard before I lost consciousness.

9th Feb\nRainy\n\nBeing alone, being able to listen to my own heartbeat makes me feel at ease.\n\nIt helps me forget my worries, and my fatigue.
13th Feb\nSunny\n\nClass discussion for today: what Kiriko will do with the prize money.\nI run because I enjoy it, not because of the fame.\n\nInstead of facing those golddiggers, I prefer hanging out at that cozy little cafe. The one right by the corner of the street.
14th Feb\nOvercast\n\nSometimes, my body starts to move before my brain.\nMade a new friend after I helped her chase down a thief who stole her purse.\nShe claims I saved her life.What an overstatement.\n\nCan't tell what goes through the mind of these rich princesses...what a bother.

Ford\n\nMale\nAged 31\nDate of Birth: 23rd Jan
28th Feb, Port B\n\nLate again. But compared to that four-month voyage, this is nothing. Olivia is on the balcony, waiting for me with a glass of ice-cold champagne. Sorry, my love.\n\nShould I do it now? The proposal.\nI could feel the ring in my pocket.\n\nPerhaps I should wait until the next voyage is over.
20th Mar, North Pacific.\n\nThis place is said to be an abandoned nuclear test site.\nThe captain promised to let me off after this "goods" delivery. When he said that, I could see a smirk from the corners of his mouth. Within the past few weeks, a strange sickness swept through the crew. Including me. The doctor only asked me to rest in bed, what a quack.
28th Mar, Unknown waters.\n\nWe should be nearing Yokohama by now, but we haven't heard from anyone for 3 days.\nI struggled to leave the cabin on my own.\nJesus, everyone has gone missing.\nNo satellite signals, disoriented compass, wonky GPS.\nWhat the hell is happening?
29th Mar, South Pacific?\n\nDear Ford, I always knew you were exceptional.\n\nWhen I stepped into the cabin, all the monitors lit up.\n\nI have a gift for you, you should prepare to go for a short trip.\nUnwrap your gift when you get there.\nThe monitor shows a course for Point Nemo.
2nd Apr, South Pacific.\n48° 52.6' S 123° 23.6' W\nIt was as if I saw a thousand suns rise across the skyline.\nScarlet skies, sweeping waves...\nWhen I woke up, it was pitch black.\nI felt the ticking of a device.\n\nHe survived. \nUnbelievable.
Hello Ford. Welcome to Purgatory. Will you be the last man standing?

Daily Mission
Players can receive Rank Tokens by surviving for a period of time in ranked mode.
The amount of tokens received will increase based on survival time. There is a seasonal limit on the amount of tokens received. Also, players will receive Ranked Tokens when they rank up.
Ranked Tokens can be used to exchange for exclusive rewards in the Ranked Store. However, please be aware that some equipment will have rank requirements.
Unused Ranked Tokens can be carried on to the next season. Ranked Store will continue to release all kinds of rewards.

Rules
Death Uprising is a round based game mode.
Zombies will spawn every rounds. Boss will spawn after a few rounds.
Lucky Card will appear after each win. There is a chance of getting rare items.
The more damage you deal, the better prizes you get in Lucky Draw
Purchase weapon skins from the store can greatly increase your firepower.
